    52	const ALG = 'sha256';
    53	const VERSION = 'aegis-attest-v1';
    54	
    55	/**
    56	 * The key never enters the repository. It is read from the environment first
    57	 * (so CI can inject one), then from a gitignored file, and is generated with
    58	 * 0600 permissions if neither exists.
    59	 */
    60	function loadKey({ create = true } = {}) {
    61	  if (process.env.AEGIS_ATTESTATION_KEY) {
    62	    const k = process.env.AEGIS_ATTESTATION_KEY;
    63	    if (k.length < 32) throw new Error('AEGIS_ATTESTATION_KEY is shorter than 32 characters');
    64	    return { key: k, source: 'env:AEGIS_ATTESTATION_KEY' };
    65	  }
    66	  if (fs.existsSync(KEY_FILE)) {
    67	    const k = fs.readFileSync(KEY_FILE, 'utf8').trim();
    68	    if (k.length < 32) throw new Error(`${path.relative(ROOT, KEY_FILE)} is shorter than 32 characters`);
    69	    return { key: k, source: path.relative(ROOT, KEY_FILE) };
    70	  }
    71	  if (!create) throw new Error('no attestation key available');
    72	  const k = crypto.randomBytes(48).toString('base64url');
    73	  fs.writeFileSync(KEY_FILE, k + '\n', { mode: 0o600 });
    74	  try { fs.chmodSync(KEY_FILE, 0o600); } catch { /* best effort */ }
    75	  return { key: k, source: path.relative(ROOT, KEY_FILE), generated: true };
    76	}
    77	
    78	/** Stable digest of an arbitrary value — key order must not change the result. */
    79	function canonical(v) {
    80	  if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') return JSON.stringify(v === undefined ? null : v);
    81	  if (Array.isArray(v)) return '[' + v.map(canonical).join(',') + ']';
    82	  return '{' + Object.keys(v).sort().map((k) => JSON.stringify(k) + ':' + canonical(v[k])).join(',') + '}';
   118	/** The exact bytes an attestation covers. */
   119	function attestationPayload(rec, pktDigest) {
   120	  return canonical({
   121	    v: VERSION,
   122	    subjectSha256: rec.reviewOf && rec.reviewOf.diffSha256,
   123	    changedPaths: (rec.reviewOf && rec.reviewOf.changedPaths) || [],
   124	    packetId: rec.packetId,
   125	    packetDigest: pktDigest,
   126	    reviewer: rec.reviewer,
   127	    reviewerModel: rec.reviewerModel,
   128	    disposition: rec.disposition,
   129	    // Findings are covered so severities cannot be downgraded after the fact.
   130	    findings: (rec.findings || []).map((f) => ({
   131	      severity: f.severity, file: f.file, problem: f.problem,
   132	      evidence: f.evidence, status: f.status,
   133	    })),
   134	  });
   135	}
   136	
   137	function sign(rec, opts = {}) {
   138	  const { key, source } = loadKey();
   139	  const pkt = opts.packetPath || resolvePacketForRecord(rec);
   140	  const pd = packetDigest(pkt);
   141	  const payload = attestationPayload(rec, pd);
   142	  const mac = crypto.createHmac(ALG, key).update(payload).digest('hex');
   143	  return {
   144	    ...rec,
   145	    attestation: {
   146	      v: VERSION, alg: `HMAC-${ALG.toUpperCase()}`,
   147	      packetDigest: pd,
   148	      payloadDigest: digest(payload),
   149	      mac,
   150	      signedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   151	      keySource: source,
   152	      scope: 'Proves this record was produced by this machine\'s adapter and is unmodified. Does NOT prove human approval and does not defend against a process that can read the key.',
   153	    },
   154	  };
   155	}
   156	
   157	function verify(rec, opts = {}) {
   158	  if (!rec || !rec.attestation) {
   159	    return { ok: false, code: 'ATTESTATION-MISSING', reason: 'the record carries no attestation. An unsigned record is not review evidence — anyone can write JSON into reviews/.' };
   160	  }
   161	  const a = rec.attestation;
   162	  if (a.v !== VERSION) return { ok: false, code: 'ATTESTATION-VERSION', reason: `unknown attestation version ${a.v}` };
   163	
   164	  let key;
   165	  try { key = loadKey({ create: false }).key; }
   166	  catch (e) { return { ok: false, code: 'ATTESTATION-NO-KEY', reason: `cannot verify: ${e.message}. Refusing to accept an unverifiable record.` }; }
   167	
   168	  const pkt = opts.packetPath || resolvePacketForRecord(rec);
   169	  const pd = packetDigest(pkt);
   170	
   171	  // Finding #3: if the packet's authorizing content changed since signing, the
   172	  // review no longer describes the permissions it was granted under.
   173	  if (a.packetDigest && pd && a.packetDigest !== pd) {
   174	    return {
   175	      ok: false, code: 'ATTESTATION-PACKET-CHANGED',
   176	      reason: 'the task packet\'s authorization content changed after this review was signed. The review no longer describes what the packet permits.',
   177	    };
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  const payload = attestationPayload(rec, a.packetDigest);
   181	  const expect = crypto.createHmac(ALG, key).update(payload).digest('hex');
   182	  const got = String(a.mac || '');
   183	  const eq = got.length === expect.length &&
   184	    crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(got, 'hex'), Buffer.from(expect, 'hex'));
   185	  if (!eq) {
   186	    return {
   187	      ok: false, code: 'ATTESTATION-INVALID',
   188	      reason: 'the attestation does not match the record contents. A covered field (subject, packet, reviewer, model, disposition, or findings) was changed after signing.',
   189	    };
   190	  }
   241	  const stageFor = (id) => {
   242	    switch (id) {
   243	      case 'spec':
   244	        if (has(/ENGOS-SPEC-UNPINNED/)) return { state: 'FAILED', reason: 'a source of truth is neither pinned nor marked UNVERIFIED' };
   245	        if (/spec pin:/.test(observed)) return { state: 'PASS', reason: observed.match(/spec pin: [^|]*/)[0].trim() };
   246	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'no spec pin was evaluated' };
   247	      case 'build':
   248	        // CONFIRMED FINDING (codex REV-…-codex, CRITICAL/HIGH set, #8): this
   249	        // returned PASS unconditionally. A diff existing is not evidence that
   250	        // it builds — nothing in a gate verdict speaks to compilation at all.
   251	        // Rendering PASS here was the projector fabricating a positive state,
   252	        // which is precisely what the amendment forbids.
   253	        if (!parsed.subject || !(parsed.subject.subjectPaths || []).length) {
   254	          return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: 'no subject paths — there is nothing here to build' };
   255	        }
   256	        if (/deterministic checks executed here: (\d+)\/\1 passed/.test(observed)) {
   257	          return { state: 'PASS', reason: 'the gate executed the declared checks and they passed' };
   258	        }
   259	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'a subject diff exists, but nothing in this verdict verifies it builds' };
   260	      case 'deterministic':
   261	        if (has(/ENGOS-DETERMINISTIC-FAILED/)) return { state: 'FAILED', reason: 'a declared check failed' };
   262	        if (/deterministic checks executed here/.test(observed)) return { state: 'PASS', reason: 'checks executed by the gate' };
   263	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'the gate did not run the checks (no --run-checks); they may have passed elsewhere, but this is not evidence of it' };
   264	      case 'codex':
   265	      case 'grok': {
   266	        const req = (parsed.classification && parsed.classification.requiredReviewers) || [];
   267	        if (!req.includes(id)) return { state: 'NOT_REQUIRED', reason: `not required for lane ${parsed.classification ? parsed.classification.lane : '?'}` };

   101	const exists = (root, rel) => fs.existsSync(path.join(root, rel));
   102	
   103	function auditSource() {
   104	  return {
   105	    prereqs: PREREQS.map((d) => ({ ...d, present: exists(SOURCE_ROOT, d.p) })),
   106	    osFiles: OS_FILES.map((p) => ({ p, present: exists(SOURCE_ROOT, p) })),
   107	  };
   108	}
   109	
   110	function auditTarget(target) {
   111	  return {
   112	    isDir: fs.existsSync(target) && fs.statSync(target).isDirectory(),
   113	    isGit: fs.existsSync(path.join(target, '.git')),
   114	    prereqs: PREREQS.map((d) => ({ ...d, present: exists(target, d.p) })),
   115	    osFiles: OS_FILES.map((p) => ({ p, present: exists(target, p) })),
   165	  console.log('Source is complete. An install would copy the full required package.');
   166	  return EXIT_PASS;
   167	}
   168	
   169	function cmdInstall(args) {
   170	  if (!args.target) { process.stderr.write('\n[install] --target <dir> is required\n'); return EXIT_USAGE; }
   171	  const target = path.resolve(args.target);
   172	
   173	  if (path.resolve(target) === path.resolve(SOURCE_ROOT)) {
   174	    process.stderr.write('\n[install] REFUSED: target is the source repo.\n');
   175	    return EXIT_REFUSED;
   176	  }
   177	  if (!fs.existsSync(target) || !fs.statSync(target).isDirectory()) {
   178	    process.stderr.write(`\n[install] REFUSED: target is not an existing directory: ${target}\n`);
   179	    return EXIT_REFUSED;
   180	  }
   181	
   182	  // Refuse on an incomplete source rather than produce a half-installed gate.
   183	  const src = auditSource();
   184	  const missing = [...src.prereqs.filter((d) => !d.present), ...src.osFiles.filter((d) => !d.present)];
   185	  if (missing.length) {
   186	    process.stderr.write('\n[install] REFUSED — the source repo is missing required files:\n');
   187	    for (const d of missing) process.stderr.write(`    ${d.p}${d.why ? `  (${d.why})` : ''}\n`);
   188	    process.stderr.write('\nA partial install of a gate is indistinguishable from a working one\nuntil it matters. Nothing was copied.\n');
   189	    return EXIT_REFUSED;
   190	  }
   191	
   192	  const all = [...PREREQS.map((d) => d.p), ...OS_FILES];
   193	  const wrote = [];
   194	  const skipped = [];
   195	  for (const rel of all) {
   196	    const from = path.join(SOURCE_ROOT, rel);
   197	    const to = path.join(target, rel);
   198	    if (fs.existsSync(to) && !args.force) { skipped.push(rel); continue; }
   199	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(to), { recursive: true });
   200	    fs.copyFileSync(from, to);
   201	    try { fs.chmodSync(to, fs.statSync(from).mode); } catch { /* best effort */ }
   202	    wrote.push(rel);
   203	  }
   204	
   205	  console.log('ENGINEERING OS — INSTALL');
   206	  console.log('='.repeat(64));
   207	  console.log(`target: ${target}`);
   208	  console.log(`copied : ${wrote.length}`);
   209	  for (const w of wrote) console.log(`  + ${w}`);
   210	  if (skipped.length) {
   211	    console.log(`kept   : ${skipped.length} existing file(s) left untouched (use --force to overwrite)`);
   212	    for (const s of skipped) console.log(`  = ${s}`);
   213	  }
   214	  console.log('');
   215	  console.log('NEXT — none of this is done for you, and none of it is claimed:');
   216	  console.log('  1. Verify:  node builder-control/test/engineering-os.test.cjs');
   217	  console.log('               node builder-control/test/tool-router.test.cjs');
   218	  console.log('  2. Copy the "Engineering OS —" steps into the target CI workflow.');
   219	  console.log('  3. Review protected-paths.json: it was copied from the source repo');
   220	  console.log('     and lists SOURCE paths. It must be rewritten for the target, or');
   221	  console.log('     the target will protect files it does not have and miss its own.');
   222	  console.log('  4. Reviewer binaries are machine-local and were NOT installed.');
   223	  console.log('  5. The attestation key is machine-local and was NOT copied.');
   224	  console.log('     The first signed review generates one at builder-control/.attestation-key');
   225	  console.log('     Add that path to the target .gitignore before signing anything.');
   226	  console.log('     Run: node builder-control/review-adapters.cjs --doctor');
   227	  return EXIT_PASS;
   228	}
   229	
   230	function parseArgs(argv) {
   231	  const a = {};
   232	  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
   233	    const t = argv[i];
   234	    if (t === '--target') a.target = argv[++i];
   235	    else if (t === '--install') a.install = true;
   236	    else if (t === '--doctor') a.doctor = true;
   237	    else if (t === '--force') a.force = true;
   238	    else if (t === '--json') a.json = true;
   239	  }
   240	  return a;
   241	}

   243	if (require.main === module) {
   244	  const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
   245	  let code;
